<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        //&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let selectedCategoryElement = $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;]&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let visibleCards = 20;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del> </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            visibleCards = 6;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        function renderCards(cardsArr) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const container = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-card-container&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            container.innerHTML = &#x27;&#x27;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const showMoreBtn = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-companies-show-more&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const cardsToShow = cardsArr.slice(0, visibleCards);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            cardsToShow.forEach(card =&gt; {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                const cardElement = document.createElement(&quot;div&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                cardElement.className = &quot;partner-card__item&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                cardElement.innerHTML = `</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-icon&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;img src=&quot;${card.img}&quot; alt=&quot;${card.title}&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;/div&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-info&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-small font-bold&quot;&gt;${card.title}&lt;/p&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-xsmall&quot;&gt;${card.company}&lt;/p&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;/div&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                `;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                container.appendChild(cardElement);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            });</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            if (visibleCards &gt;= cardsArr.length) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            } else {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;flex&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        function calculateCategory() {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let all = partnerCards.length;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let furniture = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let appliances = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let automobileIndustry = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let medicalServices = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let courses = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let solarSystems = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let other = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let touristServices = 0;</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                &#x27;ց&#x27;: &#x27;ц&#x27;, &#x27;ու&#x27;: &#x27;у&#x27;, &#x27;փ&#x27;: &#x27;п&#x27;, &#x27;ք&#x27;: &#x27;к&#x27;, &#x27;և&#x27;: &#x27;ев&#x27;, &#x27;օ&#x27;: &#x27;о&#x27;, &#x27;ֆ&#x27;: &#x27;ф&#x27;</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        $(&quot;#partner-companies-show-more&quot;).on(&quot;click&quot;, function() {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            showMoreCards();</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        });</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        // mobile filter select</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if($(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).click(function () {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(&quot;#goToTop&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(&quot;#section9&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(this).toggleClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).toggleClass(&quot;active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            });</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(document).mouseup(function (e) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if ($(e.target).closest(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).length === 0) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer__select .custom-dropdown&quot;).removeClass(&quot;custom-dropdown__is-active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    $(&quot;.filter-mobile-drawer&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    $(&quot;#topbar&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    $(&quot;#chatbot-chat&quot;).removeClass(&quot;hide-element&quot;);</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let selectedCategoryElement = $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;]&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        let visibleCards = 20;</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        if(document.body.clientWidth &lt;= 576) {</del></span>

<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        function renderCards(cardsArr) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const container = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-card-container&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            container.innerHTML = &#x27;&#x27;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const showMoreBtn = document.getElementById(&quot;partner-companies-show-more&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const cardsToShow = cardsArr.slice(0, visibleCards);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            cardsToShow.forEach(card =&gt; {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                const cardElement = document.createElement(&quot;div&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                cardElement.className = &quot;partner-card__item&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                cardElement.innerHTML = `</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-icon&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;img src=&quot;${card.img}&quot; alt=&quot;${card.title}&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;/div&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;div class=&quot;partner-card__item-info&quot;&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-small font-bold&quot;&gt;${card.title}&lt;/p&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                        &lt;p class=&quot;paragraph-xsmall&quot;&gt;${card.company}&lt;/p&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    &lt;/div&gt;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                `;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                container.appendChild(cardElement);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            });</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            if (visibleCards &gt;= cardsArr.length) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            } else {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                showMoreBtn.style.display = &quot;flex&quot;;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        function calculateCategory() {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let all = partnerCards.length;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let furniture = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let appliances = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let automobileIndustry = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let medicalServices = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let courses = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let solarSystems = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let other = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let touristServices = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let sport = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let constructionSupplies = 0;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            partnerCards.map((item) =&gt; {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;furniture&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    furniture++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;appliances&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    appliances++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;automobile-industry&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    automobileIndustry++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;medical-services&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    medicalServices++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;courses&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    courses++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;solar-systems&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    solarSystems++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;other&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    other++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;tourist-services&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    touristServices++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;sport&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    sport++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                if(item.category.includes(&quot;construction-supplies&quot;)) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                    constructionSupplies++;</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            });</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            </del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;all&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(all);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;furniture&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(furniture);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;appliances&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(appliances);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;automobile-industry&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(automobileIndustry);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;medical-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(medicalServices);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;courses&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(courses);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;solar-systems&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(solarSystems);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;other&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(other);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;tourist-services&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(touristServices);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;sport&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(sport);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;[data-category=&#x27;construction-supplies&#x27;] .notification-badge&quot;).text(constructionSupplies);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>        function filterByCategory(element) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            $(&quot;.pills-tab__item&quot;).removeClass(&quot;active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            element.addClass(&quot;active&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            const selectedCategory = element.data(&quot;category&quot;);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            if(selectedCategory === &quot;all&quot;) {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                filteredCards = partnerCards.map(item =&gt; item);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                renderCards(filteredCards);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            } else {</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                filteredCards = partnerCards.filter((item) =&gt; item.category.includes(selectedCategory));</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>                renderCards(filteredCards);</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            }</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            searchPrivileges($(&quot;.privileges__search-input&quot;).val().trim());</del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del></del></span>  
<span style="background:#ffe6e6;color:#b42318;padding:0.08em 0.18em;"><del>            let mobileSelectIcon = element.find(&quot;svg&quot;).clone();</del></span>
